2.5 UFOlogy and ufological studies
--- seg-70824ed00dc2842974a700e2 [needs-review] ---
Ufology studies so-called UFOs - Unidentified Flying Objects, or, in English, Unidentified Flying Objects (UFO), or, alternatively, Unidentified Aerial Objects (UAO) and any type of unidentified or officially described extraterrestrial movement or activity. Ufology is an independent investigative field that does not constitute a scientific research area or employ scientific methods in its investigations or descriptions.

The word ufology derives from the combination of the acronym -ufo from the English "unidentified flying objects" and the suffix -logia from ancient Greek -λογία, meaning study or field of knowledge.

The acronym UFO was coined by Edward J. Ruppelt, a captain in the United States Air Force and head of the Blue Book Project. In his 1956 book The Report on Unidentified Flying Objects, Ruppelt stated that UFO was an official term he had created to replace the popular expression "flying saucer." In Brazil, the term ufology is widely used, although the acronym UFO is commonly replaced by OVNI, and not by the equivalent term in European Portuguese, ovnilogia.

The term "flying saucer" emerged in 1947, derived from "flying saucer." In July 1947, several newspapers reported sightings of what was initially referred to as a "flying saucer," "UFO," or "hovering saucer." "Flying saucer" was translated in Brazil as "Disco Voador," in Italian as "disco volante," in Spanish as "plato volador," and in French as "soucoupes volantes." The term is said to have been created by pilot Kenneth Arnold on June 24, 1947, who observed nine aircraft flying from north to south in the U.S. sky at an altitude of 3,200 meters, appearing to be connected. He described the objects as resembling "saucer jets," made of a silvery metal that gleamed in the sun.

These strange objects were associated with extraterrestrial aircraft that were not linked to Earth. If there were spacecraft from another planet, they would certainly be inhabited by beings from outside Earth, or "ET - Extra Terrestrial."

Previously). The pilot is considered the author of the first report of a flying saucer sighting in the United States. The term flying saucer itself is an adapted translation from the English term used for the first time by the *East Oregonian* newspaper in its edition of June 25, 1947, when the paper published an interview with Kenneth Arnold's account that the vehicles he saw flew like a "saucer skipping across water." In Brazil, the official name for flying saucers became OVNI, an acronym for Unidentified Flying Object.

It is within this context that Ufology emerged to study these unexplained phenomena from human logic.

One of the most famous cases in global Ufology is the Roswell Incident, which occurred in New Mexico, United States. In July 1947, a fragment of a balloon was reportedly recovered on a ranch near the city of Roswell, New Mexico.

On July 8, 1947, Walter Haut, the spokesperson for Roswell Air Force Base commanded by Colonel William Blanchard, issued the following press release:

The various rumors about the flying saucer became a reality yesterday when the information office of the 509th Bomb Group of the VIII Air Force, Roswell Military Airport, had the good fortune to come into possession of a saucer, thanks to the cooperation of a local farmer and the Chavez County Sheriff's office.

The flying object landed on a farm near Roswell during the previous week. As he had no telephone, the farmer kept the saucer until he could contact the sheriff's office, which in turn notified Major Jesse A. Marcel of the Information Office of the 509th Bomb Group.

Immediate steps were taken to retrieve the saucer from the farmer's residence. Examined at Roswell Air Force Base, it was later turned over by Major Marcel to the competent authorities (Wikipedia, Roswell Incident)1.

News emerged on the front pages of local Roswell newspapers attesting that the 509th Bomb Group of the then U.S. Air Force had taken possession of the wreckage of a flying saucer. The news was met with tumult by Roswell residents. The next day after the disclosure, the U.S. Air Force denied it was a flying saucer and it was merely the remains of a weather balloon.

However, the fact spread worldwide and even today, Roswell hosts the Annual UFO Festival. Thousands of people from around the world visit Roswell to learn about the flying saucer and its crew. In 1979, Colonel Thomas J. DuBose reportedly told the press that the explanation of the weather balloon had been a cover-up of the true story, and the wreckage found had been switched to show to the journalists.

However, even before the more famous Varginha appearance in 1971, a document from the National Archives' collection in Rio de Janeiro indicated the appearance of an oval-shaped flying object over the municipality. The report was drafted by the Ministry of Aeronautics, dated March 3, 1971. In this document, which we attach below, it states: "A few months ago, a large part of the population of the city of Varginha had its attention drawn to an oval-shaped object, predominantly silver, which not only circled over the city but also hovered at certain points in the city's neighborhoods."

According to the official document, "in Vila Mendes, on Rio de Janeiro Street, the strange object remained stationary near the roof of a residence, causing a resident to lose consciousness due to the deafening noise."

November 1970. Correio do Sul newspaper.
In addition to the previous document, the National Archive, located in the city of Rio de
Janeiro, holds a documentary fund named "Unidentified Flying Objects" (UFOs). According to data
published by the National Archive itself, the UFO Fund (BR DFANBSB ARX) contains records
produced by the Brazilian Air Force Command. It comprises 743 records, including reports,
questionnaires on occurrences, correspondence, photographs, drawings, videos, audios, and
newspaper and magazine clippings regarding objects seen in the Brazilian sky over the years.
The first occurrence in the UFO Fund dates back to 1952, and the most recent one is from 2016.
The National Archive itself points out that it is important to keep in mind that UFOs do not
necessarily imply the existence of flying saucers or extraterrestrials. This designation - UFO -
is used to refer to any flying object whose origin was not immediately identified. That is, they
can be satellites, drones, comets, balloons, natural phenomena, among others.

Another case documented in the National Archive is the "Two Bridges Case." According to the National Archive, in August 1962, an event considered one of the most intriguing cases of contact with unidentified objects in Brazil occurred. Known as the "Two Bridges Case," the incident allegedly involved the contact and abduction of a miner in the district of Two Bridges, Diamantina municipality, Minas Gerais state. On the 19th and 20th days, a father and son sighted two objects inside and near their residence. After the contact with the objects, the father disappeared, causing a great commotion in the area and requiring the local police to intervene. The case also attracted the attention of the Civil Investigation Center for Unidentified Aerial Objects - CICOANI, which sent investigators to inquire about the incident. The boy reported the occurrence to police, doctors, judges, and journalists. According to the evaluation of CICOANI investigators, the boy's account could not be the product of invention, "delirium" or "hallucination," as it was "sophisticated and inconsistent with the sociocultural context."

Another case archived in the National Archive was called Extraterrestrial Reports - UFOs in Santo Antonio de Tauá (PA). According to National Archive information, illustrations were made based on a report of an observation of an unidentified flying object (UFO) that occurred on September 2, 1977, in the municipality of Santo Antonio do Tauá (PA). According to the report, the object was a luminous body, changing from yellow to red, moving in a zigzag pattern from East to West, producing a slight noise. During the months of September and October, several unidentified objects were sighted in the municipality and neighboring areas. According to a survey by the Air Force Command, between 1954 and 2000, Pará was the Brazilian state that recorded the most UFO sightings.

According to data collected by the National Archive, starting in 1968, various reports of appearances of unidentified flying objects (UFOs) began to be published in the Brazilian press, with great fanfare and generating numerous speculations. Popularly known as
--- seg-cc2cd2c156724ad893f9cfaa [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

===== PAGE 56 [page-dbdcfa200d706cd2e611773f] =====
--- seg-735b4f8247728ea173ab6976 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-7645b39932d854218f73525e [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-5538dcbf32de3608599b6830 [needs-review] ---
"Flying saucers," the objects were referred to by Brazilian authorities as "Unidentified Aerial Objects" - UAO. According to the National Archive, due to the popularity of the subject, the Brazilian government created the System for Investigation of Unidentified Aerial Objects - SIOANI, under the IV Air Zone of the Brazilian Air Force - FAB.

Critics of what they termed as "sensationalism" by the press and condemning "impostors" and "adventurers," SIOANI agents investigated the phenomenon using a scientific approach. They collected statements from observers and, in some cases, conducted investigations at observation sites. One of the most important stages of the investigation was the contact with the observer. At this stage, in addition to the statement, a psychiatric evaluation of the witness was made and their social and professional profile was drawn. Furthermore, an illustration of the observed phenomenon was made according to the statement - a spoken description of what had been sighted.

The following is a document from the National Archive's collection. The document contains two observation records. It is noted that in one of them, the observer claimed to have seen "crews," that is, extraterrestrial beings.

===== PAGE 60 [page-76dd63696abf3e02a0fd11a3] =====
--- seg-0a0d67d676669b79061a3174 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"

The photographs of the sighting were taken by the Bahia photographer Almiro Baraúna (1916-2000). The photographer was a guest of the Brazilian Navy to participate in oceanographic research on Trindade Island, off the Espírito Santo coast, to take underwater photographs. However, that day, on board the Admiral Saldanha ship, he captured four images of what would be a spacecraft flying over the island. The film was developed in an improvised laboratory on board the ship-school and at the time, it was not possible to see the object with precision.

A few days after the landing, Baraúna presented the developed and enlarged photographs to the press, which included images of the unidentified object. This is how the case gained international attention.

In 1967, nine years later, in a letter dated January 30, 1967, the photographer described to Colonel Colman von Keviczy, director of the Intercontinental UFO Network (ICUFON) in New York, how the sighting had occurred:

On January 16, 1958, the Admiral Saldanha ship-school of the Brazilian Navy was anchored in the cove of Trindade Island, about 800 miles from the Espírito Santo coast. It was around 11 a.m., the day was clear, the crew was preparing to return to Rio de Janeiro, when suddenly a group of people at the ship's stern, including the reserve aviation captain of the Brazilian Air Force, José Viegas, raised the alarm. In an instant, all those on the ship's deck, about fifty people, turned to observe a strange silver object in the shape of a plate, moving from the sea towards the island. The object made no noise, it was luminous, and it moved at times quickly, at times slowly, rising and falling, gently undulating, and when it increased speed, it left behind a white, phosphorescent trail that quickly dissipated.

During its trajectory, the object disappeared behind the Desejado Peak, and when everyone expected it to reappear on the opposite side, it returned in the same direction, stopped for a few seconds, and then shot off at incredible speed, disappearing into the horizon. During the appearance, I managed to take six photos, two of which, due to the chaos on deck, were not used; the other four show, in a reasonable sequence, the object on the horizon, approaching the island, stopped next to the peak (the best one), and finally disappearing in the distance. The film was developed about twenty minutes later, at the captain's request, who wanted to know if the photos were good. The negatives were viewed by almost the entire crew, and they were all unanimous in identifying the enlargements at the Navy's Secret Service. It should be clarified that the group of civilians was on board at the Navy's invitation to conduct underwater research and take photographs of the island's underwater fauna. The group consisted of: Chief Amilar Vieira Filho, a banker, diver, and athlete; Vice-Chief: Retired Aviation Captain José Viegas of the Brazilian Air Force; Divers: Aluizio and Mauro; Photographer: Almiro Baraúna.
--- seg-b09e822a185d8a68a30e18e6 [needs-review] ---

--- seg-0e3ac4154d01ead0224ce2c4 [needs-review] ---

All components were part, at the time of the events, of the Submarine Hunting Group of Icaraí.

Of the five, only Mauro and Aluizio did not see the object, as they were in the ship's mess hall, and when they came up, attracted by the commotion, the object had already disappeared.

According to rumors heard on board, the ship's electrical equipment stopped working during the appearance of the object; what I can affirm is that immediately after setting sail, the ship stopped three times, and the officers provided the most strange versions about the stops. When the ship stopped, the lights dimmed little by little until they went out completely. At that time, several officers went to the deck with binoculars, but the sky was already covered in clouds and nothing could be observed.

I must add that if it weren't for the indiscretion of a reporter from the "Correio da Manhã" newspaper, who took the photographic copies offered to then-president Juscelino Kubitschek, perhaps no one would have found out about this fact, as the Navy had already "questioned" me to find out how much I wanted to keep the photos from gaining any publicity. It is appropriate to clarify that all the officers I came into contact with throughout the entire inquiry were extremely kind to me, left me completely at ease, did not object to the revelation of the case. They only hinted that the sensationalism of the case could cause panic in the population, hence the interest of the Armed Forces in not giving publicity to cases of this nature.

30/01/1967, Almiro Baraúna (UFO Magazine, 31/08/2010).

The year 1986 was marked by the appearance of unidentified flying objects (UFOs) in the skies of Brazil. Not only by the famous "Official Night of UFOs" on May 19, 1986, when several objects were observed in the region of São José dos Campos (SP) and combat aircraft took off to investigate the phenomenon, but also by several other occurrences recorded in different parts of the country. In the months that followed, numerous reports of objects observed by civilians, aircraft pilots, air traffic controllers, and ship crews emerged. The document presented here refers to a UFO observation report by the crew of two Brazilian ships on the night of August 12.

In addition to the reports mentioned so far, some people claim to have photographed flying saucers and other unidentified objects over time, as exemplified by an article published in the newspaper Correio Brasiliense on May 22, 1969.

Due to the number of cases and the volume of the Archive held by the National Archive, in Rio de Janeiro, a report was made by G1 on May 22, 2020:

National Archive UFO Collection in the Spotlight in Newspaper

The National Archive's collection on Unidentified Flying Objects (UFOs) was featured in an article on the G1 Distrito Federal website, part of the Globo network, on Friday, May 22.

The National Archive's UFO Object - OVNI (BR DFANBSB ARX) Fund includes archives produced by the Brazilian Air Force Command. It comprises 743 records, encompassing reports, questionnaires on occurrences, correspondence, photographs, drawings, videos, audios, and newspaper and magazine clippings regarding objects observed in the Brazilian sky over the years.

The first occurrence in the UFO Object Fund dates back to 1952, while the most recent is from 2016. It's important to note that UFOs do not necessarily imply the existence of flying saucers.

The purpose of this item is not to exhaust the topic but rather to present some, historically and chronologically marked, narratives to show how the topic emerged and has maintained its public interest.

2.6 The Varginha ET case and the trigger event
--- seg-bbf62b17b52b3e985828f4ae [needs-review] ---
On January 20, 1996, a Saturday, young women Kátia Andrade Xavier and sisters
Liliane de Fátima Silva and Valquíria Aparecida Silva were returning home and, while cutting
through a vacant lot, they reportedly saw a creature as they crossed a plot of land between
the Jardim Andere and Santana neighborhoods in Varginha. It was around 3 PM that day when
a heavy storm had occurred. Beside a wall, the three young women spotted a creature with brown,
viscous skin as if oil had been smeared on it, large red eyes, and a prominent head with three
protrusions resembling horns. Upon seeing the unidentified creature, the girls screamed and
ran in the same direction they had come from.
Upon arriving home, they told their mother, frightened, what had happened. The mother then
returned to the same location with a neighbor, but found nothing, only trampled grass on the
ground where the girls had reported seeing the strange creature, and a strong smell of ammonia
in the air.

Franco Rodrigues, ufologists Marco Petit, Claudeir Covo, and A. J. Gevaerd (editor of Revista UFO - Unidentified Flying Object) participated in the investigations at the time. It is important to note that ufologist Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues was the main catalyst for the story of the Varginha ET. However, he distanced himself from the case a few years ago and does not grant interviews or comment on the incident or subsequent events.

The Baependi case, which occurred on May 16, 1979, was also investigated and publicized by Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues. That night, farmer Arlindo Gabriel dos Santos reportedly saw three UFOs and extraterrestrial beings. At one point, Arlindo was allegedly taken inside the ship and conversed with the ETs. Unlike the Varginha case, the possible appearance and abduction in Baependi did not gain notoriety nor create local identity and representations. Baependi remains known as the land of Nhá Chica, the Catholic nun Francisca de Paula de Jesus (1808-1895), who drives devotional and religious tourism in Baependi. Indeed, the Nhá Chica Pilgrimage is inscribed as Intangible Cultural Heritage of São Lourenço (MG).

However, before Ubirajara Rodrigues left the case, at that time, reports and descriptions were constantly in the media and buzzed in Varginha and among ufologists known to Ubirajara Franco Rodrigues. The ufologists then began interviewing people in Varginha who claimed to have seen supernatural creatures or inexplicable events, such as the sighting of aircraft similar to UFOs. Many of these interviews from that time are available online and can be easily accessed.

The ufologists identified that there was a series of events that occurred on that January 20, 1996, and also in the days before and after that Saturday. From these investigations, it was learned that there was strange military movement at the South Minas Regional Hospital and the Humanitas Hospital. In the documentary Moment of Contact, by James Fox, recently released, a military person who allegedly participated in the events and was at the Regional Hospital reportedly saw the creature.

During the interviews, the ufologists learned that on the same day, January 20, 1996, the local Fire Department received an anonymous call at 8:30 a.m. to capture a "strange animal" that was in the Jardim Andere neighborhood, the same neighborhood where the girls saw the other creature in the afternoon. The Varginha Case
A few days before January 20, 1996. A movement of UFOs is detected by the US satellite tracking system. It is discovered that the focus of the movements is Brazil, in the South of Minas Gerais. The Brazilian Armed Forces are alerted.
On January 13, 1996, a week before the main events in Varginha, ultralight pilot Carlos de Souza from São Paulo, sees a elongated, wingless artifact less than 15 km from the intersection that connects the Fernão Dias Highway to the road leading to Varginha. He visually follows the object, which seemed to be in difficulty, alternating altitude and route, with an opening in its front from which a fog emerged. Heading towards Belo Horizonte, after 10 km from the Varginha intersection, he observes that the object disappears behind a hill, at the Maiolini farm. Taking the unpaved road, he sees several metallic debris being collected in the pasture by military personnel. At the site, there were two trucks, a helicopter, and an army ambulance.
A larger piece of the damaged object, the size of a car, is placed in the truck's bed. Thousands of small pieces and debris scatter across the pasture. Carlos de Souza is approached by military personnel asking him to leave the site and "forget about what he saw."

"She saw". Returning to where she had come from, she stopped at a gas station and was again approached by two men who advised her not to discuss what she had witnessed with anyone. This testimony was not confirmed by others, remaining isolated after ufologists' investigations in farms, sites, and houses in that region (this account is from the documentary by James Fox, Moment of Contact, in 2022, described by the person who allegedly saw the ship and its wreckage).

January 20, 1996, around 1 a.m. Mrs. Oralina Augusta de Freitas looked out the window of the farm headquarters owned by Mr. Castilho, located 10 km from Varginha, on the side of the road connecting the city to Fernão Dias. The cattle burst out, running in a stampede. She observed a elongated artifact releasing a mist from a part that seemed "torn," in front of the fuselage.

She screamed for her husband, Eurico Rodrigues de Freitas, who was sleeping. Eurico also spotted the object, which hovered over the pasture at most five meters away. To cover a distance of approximately 500 meters, the object took almost forty minutes. And it disappeared after the hill, heading towards the city.

January 20, 1996, 8:30 a.m. Six firefighters from Varginha searched for a "creature" spotted by some locals first at the base of a construction in the Jardim Andere neighborhood. Later, the "creature" was seen moving slowly towards a eucalyptus grove owned by Mr. José Gomes, after the railroad, in the large pasture behind Jardim Andere.

A man, construction worker Henrique José, witnessed the entire incident from a neighbor's house terrace and later told investigators that four firefighters cornered the creature with their nets, captured it in a wooden box, and then handed it over to the military.

After about two hours of searching, firefighters located the "creature" and caught it in a net for animal capture. A troop transport truck from the School of Sergeants of Arms was also present. The "creature" was placed in a box in the truck bed, covered with cloth or canvas, and it emitted a kind of buzzing sound.

The truck sped off towards Três Corações and entered the School of Sergeants of Arms.

January 20, 1996, around 1 p.m. Mr. "X," who was walking in the area near the end of Jardim Andere and the nearby pastures, saw six men in military uniforms in a search formation, carrying automatic rifles and other weapons, exactly near the eucalyptus grove where the first capture had taken place.

Running to find a better observation point, Mr. "X" heard three rifle shots. Soon after, he saw those men climbing the terrain before the pasture, carrying two bags. One of them had something moving inside.

January 20, 1996, 3:30 p.m. Liliane Fátima Silva (16 years old at the time), her sister Valquíria Aparecida Silva (14), and Kátia Andrade Xavier (22) tried to take a shorter route to the Santana neighborhood, next to Jardim Andere. As they passed by a mechanic's shop on Benevenuto Braz Vieira Street, two blocks above the pasture and eucalyptus grove, they were surprised to see a "creature" with skin that shone as if it were greased. The "creature," with red and rounded eyes, jumping out of its orbits, without pupil or iris, crouched next to the mechanic's shop wall, slowly turning its head towards the girls. They don't remember the characteristics of its mouth and nose.

With purple veins bulging at the elbows, with long and thin arms and enormous feet, it had three protuberances in its advanced cranial box, giving the impression of...

January 20, 1996, 5:30 PM. A torrential downpour with hail scares the population. After the storm, two military personnel arrive at the site where the three girls had spotted the strange being. Something with a head, trunk, and limbs passes in front of the car and tries to hide in the grass.
Police officer Marco Eli Chereze, 23, runs and grabs the creature by the arm, placing it in the backseat of the car and taking it to the Regional Hospital. The police, firefighters, and military's movements cause hysteria at the hospital, where patients are transferred from wards and staff are instructed to remain silent.
January 21, 1996, midnight. The "being" is transferred to the Humanitas Hospital in an ambulance, without any fanfare. From Humanitas, the "material" is removed the following Monday, January 22, around 5:30 PM.
The "creature" is dead. The transport begins with the placement of the "being" in a wooden box, covered with plastic tarpaulin and placed in the bed of a troop transport truck, with its rear facing the hospital's back gate. Several military vehicles and some civilian ones make up the convoy. People dressed in white and medical masks are present. The being exudes an unbearable smell of ammonia.
The convoy heads to the city of Três Corações and inside the Sergeant's School of Arms.
January 23, 1996, 5 AM. The same convoy leaves for Campinas-SP, carrying two loads. The body removed from the Humanitas Hospital and a ventilated box containing something alive. After a stop at the Cadet Preparatory School in Campinas, other military vehicles transport the "material" to UNICAMP facilities. For a week, a team of researchers and scientists conducts analyses, examinations, and tests on the "creatures." Among them is the renowned forensic pathologist Prof. Fortunato Badan Palhares. Work is done on the "material," even in a modern laboratory. It is unknown what happens to these "beings" after Campinas.
On February 7, 1996, Marco Eli Chereze undergoes surgery to remove a pustule from his right armpit. With severe pain and the onset of paralysis, he is hospitalized and dies eight days later from septic pulmonary thromboembolism, according to the autopsy report. His blood tests positive for 8% of an unknown culture, as the police officer had been a victim of widespread infection.
On April 21, 1996, Mrs. Terezinha Gallo Clepf, in a restaurant inside Varginha's zoo, saw a strange creature, identical to the one seen by the young women, with one distinct difference: it had a kind of helmet on its head. At that time, two deer, a tapir, a jaguar, and a blue macaw mysteriously died. After the analyses, no cause for these deaths was discovered.

Official version - On March 11, 1997, Maurício Antônio Santos, 44, commander of the 24th
Military Police Battalion of Varginha, in a statement to the ESA of Três Corações, claimed
that the alleged extraterrestrial observed by the girls was, in fact, a thirty-something
Varginha citizen known as "Mudinho." Suffering from mental problems, Mudinho would
often be seen crouching in the streets of Jardim Andere, collecting cigarette butts and
other objects from the ground.
It is more likely that this citizen, probably dirty due to the heavy rains, seen crouching
next to a wall, was mistaken by three terrified girls for a 'space creature'," the commander's
statement records. As for the intense movement of firefighters and military police (he
does not mention the Army), it is attributed to the heavy rains of that day.
Ufologists argue that on the day of the ET's appearance, there was also intense traffic
of trucks from the ESA of Três Corações in Varginha. In the IPM, the military
dispute: the vehicles were indeed moved, but for maintenance at the Mercedes Benz
dealer in the city. An invoice for 432 reais for alignment and balancing services on eight
trucks was attached to the file.
In addition to stating that the rain had not yet started when the girls saw the alleged
ET, Covo argues that the Mercedes Benz dealer used to be closed on weekends. January
20, 1996, was a Saturday. Moreover, ufologists claim: in the IPM, the address of the
company "Automáco Comercial e Importadora LTDA." that appears on a note from the
Government's Integrated Financial Administration System (SIAFI) is different from the
invoice present in the investigation. All previously documented ufo cases in Brazil are
listed in a table from the Brazilian Aerospace Defense Command (COMDABRA),
which quantifies the number of records per year since 1952. Interestingly, 1996 is the year
with the highest number of ufo records: 65.
The case remains as a complex puzzle, filled with contradictions and discrepancies.

Witnesses have consistently and without contradiction stated that they saw an unidentified creature, and all indications are that their accounts are sincere and true.

However, proponents of the materiality of the Varginha case insistently argue that if it were merely another rumor or spectacle, police authorities, firefighters, the military, the government, universities, and hospitals would not be involved, giving it such importance or avoiding any clarifying statements about it.

Another summary of the Varginha Case was published by Sebastião Filho, on the website O Corvo Veloz, on January 17, 2011.

A summary of the Varginha ET Case

The Varginha Case is now known worldwide and is recognized by ufologists as the most important case in Brazil and the 21st century. Let's summarize the story: On January 13, 2006, Eurico Rodrigues de Freitas and Oralina Augusta de Freitas, around 1:30 a.m., saw a small, submarine-shaped craft, about the size of a minibus, with a large hole on one end, emitting white smoke, without noise, without lighting, and flying slowly. The craft appeared to be having flight difficulties and was flying over a farm 10 kilometers from Varginha's center.

On the same day, around 8:00 a.m., Carlos de Sousa, driving on Rodovia Fernão Dias, seemingly saw the same craft, now emitting a strange noise. Carlos followed the craft along the highway for about 20 kilometers when he realized it was descending into the middle of the forest. It took him about 30 minutes to find the location, and when he arrived, military personnel were already collecting thousands of pieces of the craft. He was asked to leave and keep quiet. If Mr. Carlos's testimony is true, the military must have been tracking the craft all night. Before crashing, it likely landed somewhere during the night, and strange creatures may have hidden in the forest.

On January 20, 1996, early in the morning, firefighters were called to capture a strange animal in the Jardim Andere neighborhood, which occurred around 10:30 a.m. The creature was taken by the Army. The capture was carried out by Varginha's firefighters. On the same day, around 3:30 p.m., young women Kátia Andrade Xavier, Liliane Fátima da Silva, and Valquíria Aparecida da Silva were returning from work when they encountered a strange creature. The location was three blocks from where the firefighters captured the first creature. They ran in fear at what they saw.

On the same day, around 8:00 p.m., the Military Police made a second capture of a strange creature, which was taken to the Regional Hospital. During the early morning of January 21, 1996, the creature was transferred to the Humânitas Hospital, where it later died. On January 22, 1996, in the late afternoon, a convoy from ESA - the Army Sergeant School - arrived to take away the remains.

of helmet on its head. At that time, two deer, a tapir, a jaguar, and a blue macaw mysteriously died.

--- seg-312740e1ce060c5785ad27b7 [needs-review] ---
10/19/2010 10:13 - Updated on 10/19/2010 10:14

The story of the Varginha alien gains a new version
According to the magazine Isto é, a military report contests the existence. A document concluded in 1997 was only now disclosed.
From G1 MG with information from EPTV. 2010.00/00:00
After 14 years, the Varginha alien case is still surrounded by controversies and sparks the curiosity of many. A Brazilian Army report, done in 1997 and only now disclosed, shows that no extraterrestrial being ever appeared in southern Minas Gerais.
The magazine ‘Isto é’ published a report on the police investigation that probed the involvement of the Army, Military Police, and Fire Department in the Varginha case. The result makes clear that there was no capture of an extraterrestrial being on January 20, 1996.
The article with the title “The official story of the Varginha alien” shows what, according to the magazine, are the only two documents produced by the government to investigate the occurrences and the accusations made against military personnel who allegedly participated in the case. The inquiry opened and concluded in 1997 has 357 pages and details testimonies. According to the report, the inquiry’s head, Lieutenant Colonel Lúcio Carlos Pereira, concluded that what actually happened was a misunderstanding. The text states that a citizen, probably muddy due to the rain and seen crouched next to a wall, was mistaken for a space creature by three girls. A photographic study allegedly simulated the similarity between a man and the alleged alien. A resident of the city named Luiz Antônio, now 46, was the man seen on the vacant lot at that time.
The result of an investigation conducted at the Sergeant’s School of Arms in Três Corações in May 1996 was attached to the inquiry. Ufologist Ubirajara Rodrigues from Varginha was heard in the investigation. According to him, the inquiry should be taken into account, but researchers cannot rule out hypotheses. “Every ufological phenomenon today has an entire aspect of myth, it is mythical, let’s say that. But I cannot today, after the evolution of research, affirm or deny absolutely anything about the Varginha case. We have been revisiting the case for several years and it will last for a long time,” said Rodrigues.

3. Detailed description of the cultural good
--- seg-a3953fdf94175cf2c0ab46ab [needs-review] ---
The generating event, which occurred in Varginha in January 1996, had international repercussions and made the Varginha ET Case known and generated various representations. These representations are materialized in buildings, bus stop shelters, and distinct furniture, in crafts, sculptures, animations, books, documentaries, tiles, among others.

As known among cultural heritage scholars, one of the possibilities for recognizing cultural heritage assets, both intangible and tangible, is through associations and representations made about them over time, through music, poetry, literature, images, souvenirs, cartoons, statuettes, monuments, buildings, paintings, magazine and newspaper publications, both printed and online, among others. The Varginha ET Case, as demonstrated in this study, has hundreds of representations and recreations made since 1996 until today. This makes the asset constantly alive in the collective imagination and has possibilities for continuity.

The symbolic values and images associated with an asset and that inspire distinct representations are recognized by UNESCO for the classification of an asset. One of the criteria highlighted by UNESCO for the recognition of assets and their inscription on the Representative Lists of the World Heritage is that the asset is directly or materially associated with living events or traditions, ideas, beliefs, or works of art and literature.

These associated values should project the culture and relevant aspects of the place represented. When representations of what would be aliens appear in animations, cartoons, or books, drinking coffee and eating cheese bread, it refers to the local Varginha culture and Brazilian culture as a whole. These representations, indeed, make Varginha recognized through them. If a person is photographed under the replica of the spacecraft installed by COPASA in the city center, it is not necessary for the person to describe where they are to recognize that they are in Varginha. In other words, the representation, in this case, the simulacrum of the spacecraft, takes the place of the represented thing, which in this case is Varginha and the relation with the history of the ET. The same occurs with ET representations in the form of sculptures.

Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | CEP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 91 [page-fef9aa7643803edcb2c617b7] =====
--- seg-194c434cda37f0bf884eca1e [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-7dd990c10e0ce8232a35b87e [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-332387ea11816e3ced1583e9 [needs-review] ---
The consistency of representations, speeches, and images created and recreated around the Varginha ET Case kept the memory of the event alive, even for those who have never been to Varginha. In the national and global imagination, Varginha is the municipality where the strange creatures allegedly emerged in January 1996, identified by ufologists as extraterrestrial beings. In this sense, the ET is from Varginha, it is in Varginha, even if the supposed appearance event only happened once. It's as if Varginha's territory is the territory of the ETs, or at least those that allegedly emerged there. It is very likely that the mystery and secrecy imposed by the authorities around the case, especially the military, fuels imagination, and new representations and narratives are created over time. This gives continuity to the good and promotes its permanence in the local, national, and international imagination.

Based on this idea of representations and how the Varginha ET case materialized in the municipality and outside of it, generating identity, recreations, and possibilities of continuity, we present these materializations.
--- seg-e93d1bd8cf82c9732ff68e42 [machine-unreviewed] ---
1) COPASA water tank - Elevated water reservoir
Praça Marechal Floriano, Centro, Varginha - MG
--- seg-a30683dfe40a6f7368447bc5 [needs-review] ---
The most iconic feature related to the ET Case is a massive water reservoir suspended, built by COPASA in 2003, in Praça Marechal Floriano, in the urban center of Varginha. The project for the work dates back to 2001, but construction began in 2003. The structure consists of a cylindrical column supporting a spaceship, similar to UFO representations. Dimensions of the suspended reservoir: height = 20 meters and water storage capacity of 100,000 liters. The project is by architect Paulo Eduardo Antunes Corrêa.

In the square where the reservoir was installed, a garden was created around it, and a sculpture depicting an ET was placed. The sculpture was made by plastic artist Michele Tommaso Vanzetti, interviewed during the research process for registration. Michele told us that the sculpture/statue of the so-called "ETzinho" was based on a drawing by cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio de Carvalho.

The initiative to have the "ET's Spaceship Monument" built, as the reservoir became known, was then-Mayor Mauro Tadeu Teixeira (1953-2010), who served two consecutive terms from 2001 to 2008. With the need for a water reservoir by COPASA, the municipality's water supplier, Mayor Mauro Teixeira negotiated with the company, using the Varginha ET Case story in a clever way, and thus built the reservoir with shapes resembling a large spaceship. He perhaps could not have imagined that the suspended water reservoir, in the shape of a spaceship, would become the most visited and photographed monument related to the Varginha ET Case.

The construction work of the water reservoir took place in the year 2003. The contracted contractor was COMIN Engenharias, supervised by COPASA and the Municipal Municipality as a partner. In 2005, the unusual water tank received lighting. The Monument has special nighttime lighting, creating a very futuristic effect, giving the impression of movement and color change, as in a science fiction. From almost all points in the city, it is possible to visualize the large Ship suspended in the air.

The Monument stands out both for the local population and for tourists who,
often take pictures of it or under the famous ship. The structure is located in
the center of the urban area of the municipality, near Getúlio Vargas Square, in
Marshal Floriano Square, the highest point of Major Venâncio Avenue, one of the main
access routes to the city. The asset has been listed as a Heritage of Varginha since 2010.

2) Little ET, a character created by Paulo Antônio de Carvalho
--- seg-63fe1d7120e41ed2732327da [needs-review] ---
The image of the green-colored alien, and in a more playful manner, was first created by cartoonist Paulo Antônio de Carvalho. Indeed, the name ETzinho (Little ET) is a trademark of the cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio. It is his drawings of the green-colored ET that were reproduced as sculptures alongside the ship, or water reservoir of COPASA, and other sculptures scattered throughout the municipality.

This representation of the alien by Paulo Antonio de Carvalho is one of the most famous and widespread both within and outside the municipality of Varginha.

3) Character: Ginho, the Varginha ET and the Comic Book Ginho
--- seg-df8a31cc8542b35c770778a5 [needs-review] ---
Ginho, a fictional character representing the Varginha Alien, was created in 2007 by cartoonist Márcio Baraldi for Revista UFO. It was Mário Baraldi who designed the Alien in a green representation, instead of the brown representation described by the girls who claimed to have seen the creature. The idea was to make the ET character more playful and to add a touch of humor to the seriousness of the UFO magazine.
In October 2016, the cartoonist launched the album Ginho, the Varginha Alien, in partnership between Revista UFO and GRRR (Gibi Raivoso Radical e Revolucionário), an independent label by the author.

4) E.T. Memorial in Varginha
In 2022, the E.T. Memorial was inaugurated, built on Avenida Maria Paiva Pinto, number 105 in Vila Paiva. The Memorial was constructed in the form of an architectural oval structure, resembling a representation of an UFO. In the same year, the Tourism Secretariat was relocated to the new building.

An astronomical planetarium was also constructed nearby, mimicking the shape of an UFO. Additionally, a water reservoir in the form of a rocket was built. The Memorial and Planetarium are open to the public every day of the week, from 9 a.m. to 6 p.m., with the last session at the planetarium starting at 5:30 p.m.

At the time of the inauguration in November 2022, the following note was published on Madeira's Blog:

"Inauguration of the E.T. Memorial will be next Tuesday"

After years of waiting, the E.T. Memorial will finally be inaugurated next Tuesday, November 8, after 13 years. The project was announced in 2008 with an initial investment of R$ 1.2 million. Initially, the delivery deadline was set for 2013, but it suffered several extensions. Construction was even interrupted when the responsible company went bankrupt. Currently, the building houses the Municipal Tourism Secretariat, which is expected to remain there after the inauguration.

The upper part of the Memorial is intended to house a UFOlogy museum, which, according to the Tourism Secretariat, will feature interactive panels, monitors telling the story of the case, and an observatory with a telescope.

The Memorial is located on top of Vila Paiva, near Copasa.

The project for the construction of the E.T. Memorial was announced in 2008, with funds secured directly from the Ministry of Tourism. In 2010, the city hall selected the land for construction. In 2011, the responsible contractor declared bankruptcy, and the work was halted. It was resumed in 2013/2014. The project is by architect Salvador Homero Maia Silva, a former employee of Varginha City Hall.

At the time of the completion of the works in 2021, Mayor Vérdi Melo stated:

"The completion of the Memorial's work celebrates a project carried out by many hands,
an ambitious and long-awaited project by the population of Varginha and other
states, even from abroad, as it is directly related to the E.T. case, one of the most important UFO events in the world."

The Varginha Gazette Journal, dated November 14, 2022, reported on the Memorial: "Tourists visiting the ET Memorial in Varginha are registered in a local book, where a large number of visitors from around the world have been noted. To enhance the initial project, the Secretary of Tourism and Commerce, Barry Charles Sobrinho, envisioned bringing the Planetarium that is now attached to the Memorial, where educational and cultural films with references to the ET, galaxies, and ufology will be shown for children and adults. The films will be displayed in 360° and will last up to 30 minutes. Also present at the unveiling of the ET Memorial were the young women who saw the ET in the afternoon of January 20, 1996. The unveiling of the ET Memorial is a demonstration of respect for the public resources invested there, which was not insignificant. Federal and municipal resources. The construction was controversial from the beginning, either due to the purpose given to the resource or due to the delay and suspicions surrounding the work. But finally, the work was completed and the site can have various utilities and still has the planetarium that can be used by various secretariats such as culture and education."

The ET Memorial was named 'Radialista Galvão Conde' in honor of the journalist who passed away at 84 years old in 2017. Galvão Conde dedicated himself to communication and promoting Varginha's name on radios.

5) Varginha City Hall Building
The building that houses the Varginha City Hall was also constructed with elements
alluding to the ET Case. Located at Rua Júlio Marcelini, n. 50, Bairro Vila Paiva, the
Mauro Tadeu Teixeira Administrative Center - Varginha Municipal Hall was inaugurated on
June 15, 2012. The name is a tribute to the late former mayor who served from 2001 to
2008 and passed away in 2010. The Administrative Center or Municipal Hall is located in
the former CBC - heavy industries facilities, where the SEMEL sports center and the
Department of Public Works are also located. The architect responsible for the panoramic
elevator and the dome was Giovani Oliveira Bernardes.

6) ET Square
ET Square, as the José de Rezende Paiva Square is known, located in the central area of Varginha, has two representations of approximately 2 meters in height made from the descriptions of the ET given by the girls who reportedly saw the creature in 1996. The statues impress everyone who passes through the city due to their size and type of representation.
The sculptor and prosthetist Mauri Bomfim told us that the design is his and that the execution was done by the plastic artist Cléo Nogueira. The execution is from October 2004, according to a note published in the Official Organ.

7) E.T.'s Route
--- seg-f3be37fc9b6e5da00ba24bb3 [needs-review] ---
The Varginha ET Route was part of a photographic exhibition entitled
“Photographic Exhibition: Varginha, City of the ET”, a project supported by the Aldir Blanc Law, the State Government of Minas Gerais, the Special Secretariat of Culture, and the Ministry of Tourism of the Federal Government.
In addition to the photographic exhibition, the author created a map route featuring representations of E.T.s or ships in the urban area of Varginha. The map can be accessed via the link: https://memorialdoet.com.br/rota-do-et-varginha-mapa-pontos-turisticos/
However, this Route is also used by the Tourism Secretariat to point out where works, sculptures, etc., that identify the Varginha ET Case are located in the municipality.
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | CEP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

Municipal Park Novo Horizonte
Pasteur Ave - Catanduvas Park, Varginha - MG, 37006
--- seg-a32c99b18e1c6ce54563fb72 [needs-review] ---
Located near the Zoobotanical Park and covering an area of 27,000 m² (almost three hectares), the Novo Horizonte Park was created in 1987 based on a design by architect Aristides Martins. The park features a representation of an E.T. at the entrance and a shelter for ducks near the park's lake. In 2018, there was a similar little E.T. statue to the one in Praça Marechal Floriano, but it is no longer there. The Novo Horizonte Park has been municipally protected since March 3, 2000.
--- seg-f65c13919cffded61c0bf9ec [needs-review] ---

Municipal Zoobotanical Park Dr. Mário Frota
Petrópolis Street - Petrópolis Garden, Varginha - MG, 37006-650
The Municipal Zoobotanical Park emerged in 1963, through the initiative of doctor Mário Frota, who purchased a plot of land to house the animals from the Gran African Circus. The inauguration took place in 1966, with the construction of the first animal enclosures, and in 1967, it was declared of public utility. The Zoobotanical Park has been municipally protected since March 3, 2000.
At the Varginha Zoo, it is believed that there was an appearance of E.T. shortly after the appearance to the girls in January 1996.

8) Shelters and bus stops
--- seg-d36001af0be17ea4a8082fbf [needs-review] ---
Bus stop shelters were first installed during the administration of Mayor Mauro Teixeira (2001 to 2008). The shelters also form part of the ET Route. The first shelter was installed at VTC in 2002. The company that produced the shelters was Fibras FKL, located in Varginha.

10) Local crafts based on E.T. representations
--- seg-93b6672d5fbbee91b49ee7f0 [needs-review] ---
At a meeting with the craftswomen of Varginha, gathered in local associations, namely: the
Varginha Mineira Craft Association (AMINARTE), the Varginha Association of Popular
Craftsmen and Artists (ASSOART), the Varginha Association of Retirees and Pensioners
(AAPV), and the Varginha Association for Creative Economy (AVEC), all agreed to the
Registration and were enthusiastic about the idea of patrimonial protection of the good.
The Registration will enable forms of fostering sustainable development and boosting
sales of pieces related to the Varginha ET Case. Remembering that the Convention for the
Intangible Cultural Heritage of UNESCO reaffirms "the importance of intangible cultural
heritage as a source of cultural diversity and a guarantee of sustainable development, as
highlighted in the UNESCO Recommendation on the Safeguarding of Traditional and Popular
Culture, 1989, as well as in the UNESCO Universal Declaration on Cultural Diversity, 2001,
and the Istanbul Declaration, 2002, adopted by the Third Ministerial Round Table on
Culture" (Unesco, Safeguarding Intangible Cultural Heritage, 2003).

--- seg-fbb4b5b407361eaa50964157 [needs-review] ---

11) Statues with replicas of what is imagined to be the appearance of an ET
--- seg-34d08457194becc87413f204 [needs-review] ---
Statues with replicas of the creature described by the girls were scattered throughout the city. The design of the little ET installed next to the COPASA water tank or ship was done by cartoonist and illustrator Paulo Antônio de Carvalho in a work executed by plastic artist Michele Tommaso Vanzetti.

Michele Vanzetti told us that he carved the piece on-site where it was installed, and that the head was the most challenging part to make. It was made elsewhere and later brought to the square. The head was made with fiberglass to keep it from becoming too heavy, and the rest of the body was made with cement plaster molded. The sculpture is 2 meters tall and took about two months to complete. The stone on which the sculpture was installed was donated by the Varginha Country Club and brought to the site specifically for the installation of the little ET replica.

The mayor who commissioned the work was Mauro Teixeira, and his wife, Geisa Gomes Pereira Teixeira, is said to have suggested creating a more playful model of an ET in green, distinct from the older brown representations, according to Michele Tommaso Vanzetti's account. It was during this time that they also installed the little ET from the CDCA - Child and Adolescent Development Center.

Michele also mentioned that the map of Minas Gerais positioned on the chest of the little ET was stolen twice and later recreated. The previous two replicas were made of glass. Finally, they made a replica of the Minas Gerais map using plaster and it's the one currently on-site, representing the little ET's heart.
--- seg-3922b19f46a0da44d45d6d77 [needs-review] ---

12) The sculptor Mauri Bomfim Gabriel
--- seg-0f9b87acd3a36df95b7180b8 [needs-review] ---
Sculptor Mauri Bomfim, 63, was one of the first to reproduce in miniatures representations of what is assumed to be the appearance of the Varginha ET.
--- seg-09bc37a6671650f93a86b444 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

Page 130 of 403

===== PAGE 131 [page-f4edd17dcff67dafe5b0a2ef] =====
--- seg-fef794d39734a3990e83085c [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-cbd731d9e6357dcd41181d91 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-23ea45f979beabb87dcfb1fc [machine-unreviewed] ---
Mauri told us that he is a prosthetist and that as soon as the ET story was publicized in the media,
he became interested in it. One day, shortly after the events were reported, he was in the dental prosthetics laboratory and the power went out and he could not continue his work, so he began
sculpting a representative image of the ET in plaster. Until then, he had never sculpted any kind of representation. For him, the first representation was more faithful to the descriptions of the girls. Later, he created new, more playful forms and others that appeal to all types of audiences, but always referring to the descriptions of the girls who had seen the creature.
--- seg-10f4c42d0beb2dffc1e19278 [needs-review] ---
Image 96: Early representations of the Varginha ET made by Mauri Bomfim, still in 1996. Collection of Mauri Bomfim.
--- seg-3db75911f594d72d7b91fad6 [needs-review] ---
It is possible to notice that these early representations were quite faithful to the description of the girls who saw the creature. Later, Mauri improved the pieces and made distinct models, more playful and aesthetically more accepted by society.

Mauri considers that the story of the ET "fell from the sky" for him, as from then on he started creating representations and making miniatures and full-size pieces that gained worldwide recognition. He accounts that he discovered art through the ET story. The art work based on ET representations functions as occupational therapy and to financially support his family needs. Mauri stated: "The pieces I create are generally to express something to elevate the ET's and Varginha City's name, foster tourism, the profession, and even in the education field, to debate prejudices such as racism. In addition...

Municipality of Varginha | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Sector responsible: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage

===== PAGE 132 [page-b6b5ca229ea25ec8bac203a3] =====
--- seg-d7bd832a138742f0b4cde409 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-e3f131253029d8c2e4aba0cc [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-a9ba41974c6c91a31a5f0ad0 [needs-review] ---
From this, it is possible to create versions that highlight agriculture, the environment, reading, among others."
Mauri has approximately 30 distinct models that represent the Varginha ET. The first time he molded it, it was in plaster, but he has also carved it in wood, clay, stone, metal, among others. He considers that he has experimented with all possible raw materials to create pieces that represent the Varginha ET.
He only carves and creates representations of the Varginha ET, nothing else. Mauri also told us that pieces he has made have gone to various places around the world. He even made a piece for the Roswell Museum, who took the piece was the ufologist Vitório Pacaccini.
The pieces are always made by order, and he says he doesn't always manage to meet the demand. In Brazil, he sends them to stores that order his work in other states like Bahia, São Paulo, Paraná, and cities like Taubaté, São Thomé das Letras, São José dos Campos, among others. Outside Brazil, he remembers sending pieces to Canada and the United States. Some time ago, some acquaintances approached him and said they had seen his pieces in a store in Miami, USA.
The sculptor has also made numerous pieces that were given as gifts to artists such as Faustão, Jô Soares, Sandy, Junior, Milton Nascimento, and Ana Maria Braga. Some of these artists presented the pieces he sent on their shows. One of the first to show his pieces was Ratinho on his show 26 years ago. His pieces also appeared on the program "A Fazenda."
He also created ETs that represent football teams. These are very famous and in high demand. Some coaches like Parreira and Felipão have been given pieces made by him. Mauri said it's not him who gives the gifts, it's always someone who orders and buys the pieces to give to artists and celebrities or take abroad.

Mauri Bomfim believes his action was one of those that kept the history alive not only
in the municipality of Varginha, but beyond it. He considers that he was selling, at the beginning,
around one thousand souvenirs per month. Currently, he sells around five thousand pieces per

for himself. These pieces are dressed in characteristic clothes of each era, as was the case
of the World Cup era, represented below. Other life-size versions, when commissioned, are made
for sales.

14) Games based on the Varginha Case
--- seg-a32f8e265635e54956dbbf18 [machine-unreviewed] ---
➢ Varginha Incident
--- seg-920505cf9fac73eb238b3ca0 [needs-review] ---
In September 1998, two years after the Varginha ET Case, Perceptum in partnership with the Software Company in Brazil and Midas Interactive in Europe, Asia, and other countries in South America, launched the game "Incident in Varginha." This game is considered "the first video game based on a real event in Brazil."

Municipality of Varginha | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Sector responsible: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | CEP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-b96a25a679379c70a069dad6 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 138 of 403

===== PAGE 139 [page-fe4678c36ecbfe8d3ca52762] =====
--- seg-d7ba2fc9122a259d261af9b1 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-853ad32eae49c183a7704974 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-d852f18ed7b09d0072dc2e4f [needs-review] ---
"the first major game production developed in Brazil" and "the first Brazilian FPS", as well as
being the game that gave the initial push for more elaborate national game productions.
In other countries, the game is marketed as Alien Anarchy or Misión Alien. To develop the game, the Perceptum developers (Marcos Cuzziol, Odair Gaspar) traveled to the city of Varginha, where they spoke with residents to get a firsthand account of the case. In addition to this research, they also stated that they studied ufological mythology in general so that "the backstory made sense to those familiar with the subject."
According to information from the Uol Notícias website, the game's music was composed by Fábio Cardelli, and the setting is in Brazilian cities such as Rio de Janeiro, São Paulo, and Varginha. The game was in production for two years and four months. 2,000 copies were sold in the country and 20,000 in the rest of the world. After the release, Perceptum signed an agreement with the US Special Forces Training Center and NovaLogic to use the software to train Delta Force soldiers.
--- seg-aa5c66cde4b77516999d565c [needs-review] ---
Bitnamic Software Game. Available at: https://www.bitnamic.com.br/produto/incidente-em-varginha/

16) UFO Magazines
--- seg-47a4dd84af196e1d64773b9b [needs-review] ---
Ademar José Gevaerd founded the UFO Magazine in 1985. The Magazine is considered the only one
on ufology existing in the country, with almost 40 years of duration, and the oldest in circulation
worldwide.
Since 1996, various editions of the UFO Magazine have been dedicated to publishing about the
Varginha E.T. Case. In 1996, under the title "Alien captured in Minas Gerais and authorities deny",
issue 43 published an extensive article about the appearance of a strange creature in Varginha.

18) Fiction works inspired by the Varginha E.T. Case
--- seg-92022a28f3bf0b53971d99e5 [needs-review] ---
Book: Et of Varginha. The Et of Brazil, 2016, by Arlindo Cruz
Synopsis: This work is not a UFO study of the incident. It is a retelling of it, in the category of
romantic fiction, starring two young people, one human and one extraterrestrial, who appear
naively and recklessly. Recounting the case as a love story seems impossible, something of a
lunatic. But, in the author's view, everything happened for love, and he explains why the ETs
were in Varginha, forcing them to take such risks among its inhabitants. Thus, the story begins
in the rural area of Varginha and then moves to its urban area. The plot, at first, is set in the
middle of a forest and a village of simple-minded Minas Gerais and Northeastern people.
Fictitious, it takes place on January 19, 1996, one day before the "D-day". The events resulting
from the romantic involvement between the two different young people allow them to live an
adventure and passion journey while escaping those who are pursuing them: ETs, the army,
the farmer and his gang. The urban plot, with the "D-day" and the young people's antics in
Varginha, was constructed based on witness descriptions, reports (from TV, magazines and
newspapers), documentaries, reports (official or not), ufologist books, and mainly, the contact
report of the three girls with an ET. All the facts take place in 48 hours and are retold in a
timeless and sequential manner. Everything is intertwined, without boredom, in the novel. This
work is filled with romantic scenes, plenty of action, adventure, suspense, and touches of humor.
--- seg-70b489c68febeb3a21e21048 [machine-unreviewed] ---

Book: Varginha Incident by Marcos Otero
Synopsis: WHAT IF THEY WERE AMONG US?
Nine days before one of the largest UFO events ever recorded in Brazil, on January 20, 1996, the population of the small town in the interior of Minas Gerais began to witness the first appearances of UFOs. The Army Aerospace Control Center, upon receiving news from NORAD (North American Aerospace Defense Command) that an unidentified object was about to enter Brazilian airspace, activates the agents from the SNI (National Investigation Service) known as "the dogs."
Parallel to military movements, the UFO community appoints History teacher Max Miller (who had left ufology due to personal conflicts) to investigate the events.
Max Miller discovers more clues, narrowing down a mysterious and contradictory tale, and begins to suspect that something much larger is being hidden.

Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"

YMATMFS1NSFH&keywords=varginha&qid=1682712281&s=digital-text&sprefix=varginh%2Cdigital-

--- seg-00174641f0fd81ab57382e92 [needs-review] ---
French book: Contacts ovnis au Brésil, by Thiago Luiz TICCHETTI
Synopsis: Brazil is one of the countries with the highest number of UFO cases in the world. The incidents that occur in the country are among the most spectacular in the ufology literature, and this book will present the reader with some of the most fascinating close encounter cases, many of which have never been published abroad before, with numerous details, photos, and illustrations. It covers well-known cases, such as the Varginha incident that occurred in January 1996 and which was a catalyst for the first global ufology forum in Brasilia in 1997, attended by the French author and speaker Gildas Bourdais. The book gives significant attention to the 1977 Operation Prato: from the late 1970s, especially in the Colares region, fishermen and peasants were hunted at night by small UFOs nicknamed "chupas," lifting them off the ground and burning them with light rays. A military investigation took place in the Colares region (Pará state), known as "Operation Prato" and led by Colonel Hollanda. The latter confirmed the events in 1997. A significant declassification of UFO archives was carried out by the military around 2007, concerning this case among others. The Crab Island case was the subject of a lengthy investigation by American ufologist Bob Pratt, who lived in the country for a long time. This tragic case saw a 22-year-old man lose his life on April 25, 1977, under unexplained circumstances to this day. The book also includes a complete account of the Antonio Villas-Boas case, a historical abduction case that occurred in 1957 in Minas Gerais. Also, the Ubatuba case (1957), an authentic UFO crash studied by official American services and commented on in the main ufology magazines worldwide. The book contains several other accounts of lesser-known UFO crashes.

19) Characters and versions based on the Varginha E.T.
--- seg-b1e97dc53670de2cc0441e1a [needs-review] ---
Starting in 1996, various representations were made to show how the E.T. from Varginha, who became part of the local imagination, would look.

The first representation of E.T. was made by the cinematographer of TV Alterosa, Vinício Cunha. The drawing was based on the accounts of the three girls who reportedly saw the creature, at the request of ufologist Ubirajara Rodrigues, shortly after the initial reports of the Case. Subsequently, numerous other representations were made, including those of ETzinho (Little E.T.) and Ginho (Varginha's E.T.). These became popular for presenting E.T. in a more playful way, in green, appealing more generally to children, from around 2005.

Later, dozens of other versions were created, some more serious, others more playful, some more frightening, others joyful. Some of the names that stand out in the production of these Varginha E.T. versions, and that are emphasized in this Dossier, are those of cartoonists, illustrators, and artists: Mauri Bomfim, Paulo Antônio de Carvalho, Michele Vanzetti, Mário Baraldi, Vinício Cunha, Elizabeth Aparecida Rodrigues Silva, Flávio Mattos, Gleiber Antônio Piva, among others. We apologize in advance for not including everyone.

20) Other distinct ET models from those representing Varginha
--- seg-3522c05ef086a45144ea272f [needs-review] ---
The representations of the Varginha ET are quite distinctive, which makes it unique. 21) Varginha ET becomes a character in the animated film "BugiGangue no Espaço"
In the 2017 animation "BugiGangue no Espaço" by filmmaker Ale McHaddo, the Varginha ET is one of the characters and appears, caricaturely, with boots and a Minas Gerais accent.
--- seg-ddf59bcbafa7e16ff17e4760 [needs-review] ---
Produced by 44Toons, the alien will be a treasure hunter who joins other characters on a space journey. The character's costume is quite caricatured, with boots, a yellow uniform, and a hat. According to the producer, the film will be the first Brazilian 3D animation. Also note the voice-overs that will be done by Maísa Silva and Danilo Gentili.

22) Documentaries and major reports produced
--- seg-3cab3b41c55d45c8ef309557 [needs-review] ---
The Varginha E.T. Case or Varginha Incident is one of the most persistent UFOlogy cases in the media among all Brazilian cases. Numerous articles, documentaries, movies, among others, can be found in a quick Google search. We present here a small part of this universe of journalistic or audiovisual creations that is much larger.
- Documentary “Moment of Contact” (or “Moment of Contact - The Varginha Case”), by American filmmaker and ufologist James Fox, released in 2022.

===== PAGE 188 [page-b0f41f8b9a8c5f360a369e5e] =====
--- seg-db3e41ff60ce3ab39438fccd [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-567958ae6aa8f1249ea6c5aa [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-90b762f83fd8d240aea9fe81 [needs-review] ---
In 2020, during a Heritage Education Workshop held with Varginha's Heritage Councilors, they produced perception maps. By analyzing the drawings produced by the councilors, it was noticed that two very significant cultural references repeated themselves on the maps, among which we highlight: "Varginha ET and Railway Line". From there, negotiations and ethnographic studies began to identify how the Varginha ET mobilizes the local population's imagination, for the possibility of Registration.
--- seg-4cfbcd5c2dd9e16df5cb6b11 [needs-review] ---
Image 213: Heritage Education Report. Chart QIIIC, Cultural Heritage ICMS, 2020 exercise 2022.
--- seg-bee001dae20fb35df232503b [needs-review] ---
In 2021, the educational action "Playing with Heritage" was carried out, which integrates the
"Our Heritage, Our Treasure" project. The project is a proposal from the Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage and the Municipal Public Library aimed at elementary school education. The action included a virtual lecture and video presentation that discusses the history of the city, historical, cultural, and artistic heritage, and its main concepts in an interactive way, with the participation of 5th year B students from the SESI Varginha - Unit II elementary school. The purpose was to introduce and awaken awareness of heritage in the Municipality of Varginha | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

===== PAGE 189 [page-8b86470ef648b30894037b39] =====
--- seg-182f49e528f4b78a8d489cd9 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-8dc843055d95c8b3bbe00e3a [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-81a20fcbe8ccb955c3a9b75d [needs-review] ---
students knowledge, awareness, and the need to value and preserve Varginha's cultural heritage.
The proposed theme was introduced at the school through the presentation of a lecture on heritage,
the history of the municipality, and key concepts related to the topic, such as culture,
memory, designation, heritage, and cultural assets. A digital folder providing a map of locations
and a concise history of the city's protected heritage sites was made available beforehand for
student and teacher reference.
To narrate the municipality's history, lecturer and teacher Eliana Cristina Costa showed students
the video of the doll Nina in Varginha. Thus, the lecture introduced students to the concepts of
memory, culture, identity, designation, and the importance and influence of cultural assets on
the development of the municipality, for the recovery of memory, traditions, and social and
political transformations in the city. A brief history of some of Varginha's main material assets,
such as the Railway Station, Afonso Pena State School, Dom Pedro II Square, City Hall, and the
Old Forum, was also presented to the students. Activities titled "What I Would Like to Become
Cultural Heritage" and "Drawing Varginha's Cultural Heritage" were conducted.
Throughout the presentation of the city's history, it became evident that the children were visibly
surprised by the actual facts about Varginha's history, as the incident that occurred in 1996,
involving a supposed appearance of an extraterrestrial on municipal territory, had so deeply
penetrated Varginha's culture that younger generations know little or nothing about the historical
events preceding the arrival of this remarkable folk figure and legend to the municipality.
Regarding the pedagogical activities of the Workshop, two freehand drawing activities were
conducted: "What I Would Like to Become Cultural Heritage" and another titled "Drawing
Varginha's Cultural Heritage." In the first activity, students, through freehand drawings,
selected new cultural heritage sites. By analyzing the students' drawings, we observed that the
"ET's Ship" holds significant cultural value for the students. In the second activity, students
drew some local cultural heritage sites in the municipality. The activities were adapted due to
the COVID-19 pandemic.

"Luis the Mute": the creature sighted in the interior of Minas Gerais, Brazil, in the city of Varginha on January 20, 1996. Several witnesses claimed to have seen the creature, or creatures. It is believed that the creatures were captured by the army and are still in their possession. The case became known as the Varginha ET case.

29) Zedi, mascot of the Shopping Via Café Garden in Varginha
--- seg-8b2ffd44bf9f4c981afe272c [needs-review] ---
At Via Café Garden Shopping, considered one of the largest shopping centers in South Minas,
located in Varginha, when the visitor arrives, they are greeted with a smiling sculpture that
represents the Varginha ET. It is very common to see children hugging the sculpture of the most
famous character of Varginha.
Zedi, the name given to this version of the ET, is the mascot of the Via Café Garden Shopping. The
choice was made through popular voting in 2015. With 70% of the votes, Zedi, an intelligent and
modern E.T., was elected. The other mascot, called "Dedeco," a coffee bag companion who loves
to hang out with friends and highlight the importance of the coffee-producing region, was
eliminated. Although the shopping center's name refers to coffee, it was a friendly little E.T.
that the public chose.
--- seg-99d2263ac227f396623159f0 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | ZIP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

32) The Varginha ET is also a mascot for the Varginha Esporte Clube, the VEC
--- seg-5e7a828703746f1b1130c722 [needs-review] ---
According to data from Wikipédia, the Varginha Esporte Clube, popularly known as VEC, is a Brazilian football club, from the city of Varginha, in the state of Minas Gerais. Founded on February 12, 1985, under the denomination Sport Clube Aristocrata Negreiros, it maintained its activities at the Municipal Stadium Nego Horácio, in the Vila Barcelona neighborhood. As could not be otherwise, the VEC's mascot is a representation of the Varginha ET.

===== PAGE 206 [page-0d6413b085d60d8c38511fa0] =====
--- seg-118355d5ad4811a957eddda3 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-d107c53f34e6824f382be92b [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-3689b5b43922799d6b7579a8 [machine-unreviewed] ---
its panels. And that, during his travels around the world, Henrique Elisei, native of Varginha and mentor
of Pentacústica, has always been questioned about the story of the ET. Below are information from his own website:
--- seg-dc8e3a3b856807c8668d84b3 [needs-review] ---
In his travels around the world seeking knowledge and business contacts, Henrique was always asked about the curious history of his hometown, the so-called "ET city." The topic was so prevalent that it sparked hours of conversations and debates about its peculiarities and authenticity. Not long after, friends in business occasionally started calling him an ET.

On a cold night in 2006, while working on the inscriptions for the front and rear panels of a new product, and chatting about audio in a social network group, someone suggested adding an ET design to the equipment. At first, it seemed like a joke, but the idea was immediately implemented casually, placing a Brazilian flag, a Minas Gerais flag, and another with an alien figure above the "made in brazil" inscription. The subtle symbolism, displayed in the social network group, received criticism but was acclaimed by a wide majority and became present in all products manufactured by the brand since then, eventually becoming a company icon.
--- seg-068833b0d6780f828a2a1f01 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Image 245: The first ET art used by Pentacustica, in the photo, a part of the rear panel of our extinct energy manager PC-4000.
--- seg-681db3f629baf19030b25b27 [needs-review] ---
The year 2012 was marked by great changes, the sharp growth of the company demanded the adoption of new management tools, larger production space, a team of even more prepared collaborators, and much more innovative products. Our renowned line of energy managers was undergoing a deep overhaul, and on the eve of its launch, while observing a supplier's catalog, we noticed that the buttons on its keys could serve as the eyes of an ET. We changed the design and the current symbol of our alien mascot was born from there. It still adorns the front panel of this equipment line today.
--- seg-ee9f55f8a3cf1ba2beab3b31 [needs-review] ---
Image 246: The ET symbol as it is today. In the photo, the front panel of the energy manager PM-
1.1.
