36) Main changes related to the good
--- seg-5c1607cc16a89edca54e42b7 [needs-review] ---
One of the main changes that occurred over time was the way the Varginha ET was represented and its many versions. The story, what we call the generating fact, never changed, but the representations did. Initially, the ET was represented in brown and with features similar to those described by the girls who reportedly saw the creature. The first sculptures made by Mauri Bomfim, for example, in 1996, are more faithful to the "spoken portrait" of the girls who saw the creature, as we see in the photograph of these first pieces. Later, other more playful and "friendly" versions were created, and the ET began to be represented in green as well. The reason for this change, according to those who worked on creating the Varginha ET versions, is that the Varginha ET images should appeal to and not repel children. The more playful versions, with a smiling ET or with typical rural stereotypes, of football players, with coffee, among others, attract more than the other versions, which are the most faithful to the testimonies of the girls who reportedly saw the creature. However, the two versions, or better yet, the many versions and aesthetics of the Varginha ET coexist.
--- seg-42607f9c76e9b92fbef42e5a [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | ZIP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

37) Possibilities of continuity
--- seg-88ed4134335b00acf2a803a7 [needs-review] ---
The proposed Safeguarding Plan presents actions that ensure the preservation of the good in the present and its continuity into the future. During the research, it was identified that the good is alive in local, national, and international memory and does not currently face a risk of disappearance. The continuity of the good is achieved through the natural process of diffusion carried out by the press, ufologists, and all those who have created and continue to create versions of the Varginha ET character.

4. Justifications for Registration
--- seg-9213a082646da4e3b762a3ff [needs-review] ---
The request for the registration of the Varginha ET Case as local heritage originated from the Varginha community itself, which considers it a unique cultural asset of the municipality. Therefore, there was a movement by citizens to have this asset identified and declared as local intangible heritage.

The Varginha ET Case has been perpetuated in the local, national, and international collective memory since 1996, and is constantly and daily disseminated and recreated through the press, films, and other audiovisual records, books, crafts, cartoons, games, patrimonial education, sculptures, among other aspects described in this Dossier. In addition, the asset has materialized in the urban architecture and furniture related to the Varginha ET Case in the municipality's downtown.

Initially, the event was widely publicized by the press and ufologists, but later it transcended these spaces and became used by various other individuals in the municipality, in Brazil, and even worldwide, as evidenced by the recent documentary released in the United States and produced by James Fox, "Moment of Contact" (2022). Another recent documentary was produced by Japanese TV and TV Tokyo, which is set to be released. Both documentaries were filmed in 2022 and released between 2022 and 2023.

The Varginha ET Case is considered by the World Ufology as the second most important case in the world of a supposed extraterrestrial appearance. The first case is Roswell, which occurred in July 1947 in the city of the same name, New Mexico, United States, involving the supposed appearance of an unidentified flying object.

It is important to highlight that the speech place for this case's registration is from the Varginha community itself, which recognizes the asset as local cultural heritage, as evidenced by the statements in favor of registration through signatures in the consents and petitions attached to this Dossier. The hundreds of representations created and recreated daily, which keep the memory of the asset alive, also reinforce its local recognition.

The protection derived from this registration, as strictly described in this Dossier, concerns the representations created in the municipality regarding the Varginha ET Case. The municipality of Varginha | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Head of Sector: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Sector responsible: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage

===== PAGE 223 [page-4f95f3c6a25a9b35d59f8305] =====
--- seg-c8148f4b85ddffbd4c5d6fa5 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-acdbe2b608fc230281ee2e3e [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-78bf36e1933fe7d80f853158 [needs-review] ---
The representation presented in this Register Dossier is based on the definitions of Carlo Ginsburg and Roger Chartier, following UNESCO's understanding that representations, along with practices, expressions, knowledge, and techniques, can be recognized as Intangible Cultural Heritage. To this end, a new municipal Register book, the Register Book of Representations, has been opened to inscribe representations, such as the Varginha ET. The asset is also being inscribed in the Book of Places. Furthermore, one of the possibilities for recognizing a cultural and material heritage asset is through associations and representations made about it over time, through music, poetry, literature, images, souvenirs, cartoons, statuettes, monuments, buildings, paintings, magazine and newspaper publications, both printed and online, among others. The Varginha ET Case, as demonstrated in this study, has hundreds of recreations, representations, and versions of the Varginha ET persona made since 1996 to the present day. This makes the asset constantly alive in the collective imagination and has possibilities for recreation and continuity. For UNESCO, the associated values present in the representations project the culture and
the relevant aspects of the place represented. When representations of what would be
extraterrestrials appear in animations, cartoons or books having coffee and eating cheese
bread, it refers to the local culture of Varginha and Minas Gerais, as a whole. These
representations, indeed, make Varginha recognized through them. If a person is
photographed under the replica of the spaceship, installed by COPASA in Varginha's Center,
it is not necessary for the person to describe where they are for it to be recognized that
they are in Varginha. In other words, the representation, in this case, the mockup of the
ship, takes the place of the represented thing, which in this case is Varginha and the
relationship with the history of the ET and UFOs. Varginha became known worldwide as the
city of Varginha's ET from what became known as the "Varginha Incident" or "Varginha
Incident" or, still, "Varginha ET Case." This is an undeniable fact and it is not a point of
discussion. For these reasons,
the good is also being inscribed in the Book of Places, due to the understanding that
the entire territory of Varginha is identified as “ET land”. With the disclosure and great impact of the case, the municipality of Varginha "gained" an
ET, since in all references about the alien, it appears as Varginha's ET. Not
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Chief: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage

===== PAGE 224 [page-de1403e29886254e40817caf] =====
--- seg-9640c90685b9cf74e6811373 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-d37fe2697a80aaed3b6d584b [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-e6dbaabd3a2c25a7ff4d80e2 [needs-review] ---
It is an ET in Varginha, or an ET that was in Varginha. But it is "de," a personal pronoun.
The ET is recognized as being from Varginha, including cartoons and playful videos that refer to an ET who became a miner, wears boots, likes cheese bread, and talks with the distinctive accent of the people from the interior of Minas Gerais. The representations of the ET who is "de" Varginha appear stereotyped with signs that refer to the people from the interior of Minas Gerais, whether by accent, clothing, and taste for coffee and cheese bread.
The consistency of representations, discourses, and images created and recreated around the ET case in Varginha has kept the memory of the event alive, even for those who have never been to Varginha. In the national and international imagination, Varginha is the municipality where the strange creatures allegedly appeared in January 1996, identified by ufologists as extraterrestrial beings. In this sense, the ET is from Varginha, it is in Varginha, even if the supposed appearance event only happened once. It's as if the territory of Varginha is the territory of ETs, or at least those who allegedly appeared there. It is very likely that the mystery and secrecy imposed by the authorities, especially the military, around the case fuels imagination, and new representations and narratives are created over time.
We are referring to a community that was culturally marked by a particular event, in this case, the ET incident in Varginha. When Varginha is mentioned, whether in Brazilian territory or outside, the first association made is to the ET case, as amply demonstrated in this Dossier. Although coffee is the most prominent and enduring product that previously represented the municipality, currently, the ET case projects Varginha onto Brazil and the world.
The registration as local intangible heritage will enable safeguarding actions focused on patrimonial education directed towards astronomy studies, spatial knowledge, and ufology. However, it is not about proving or asserting the existence or non-existence of extraterrestrial beings and their spacecraft. Rather, it is about recognizing a collective memory that created representations and how these representations forged a local identity, enabling hundreds of recreations and the continuity of the good. The good has persisted in the local, national, and global imagination without any impetus from the public power.
--- seg-830cfe4b158a0cb5ece0ca9c [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães

===== PAGE 225 [page-0fc162f643e08216f2dae4cc] =====
--- seg-a64139dff85ee81bbb93b52d [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-772ba32e3ea9b65b817738cc [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-56910824a90a136302ed5a19 [needs-review] ---
Varginha. The idea is that now the public power should be part of this diffusion, impulsion, and
preservation actions.
It is important to highlight that, as a cultural fact, the Varginha ET Case was forged
by the Varginha community itself, by ufologists, and by those who appropriated some way
of the good to create versions and representations that have been perpetuated in national and
world memory.
We can surely affirm that the Varginha ET Case and all the representations, narratives,
mysteries, and mythifications it triggered, have made it a good far beyond the territorial
limits of Varginha. The Varginha ET Case and everything related to it, has a national reach.
The current mayor, Vérdi Melo, stated regarding the Registry that "The ET Case made
our city world-known. Today, we receive visitors from all over, scholars of the case spread
the Varginha incident, and it is up to us, public managers, to take care of this illustrious
visitor who chose our city to land."
For José Manoel Magalhães Ferreira, president of the Municipal Cultural Heritage
Deliberative Council of Varginha, "whether or not the ET exists is not the main issue. The
fact is that this phenomenon has transformed the way to see and perceive the city." "If
someone asks me what is Varginha's identity, I believe that even coffee loses to the ET. We
are the largest coffee commercial center in Brazil and the world, but we are better known
for the ET," José Manoel reinforces.
In the municipality, there is a unanimous opinion that the Varginha ET Case has
indelebilly marked the municipality's identity and the way it is seen and represented.
Indeed, children demonstrate this understanding through drawings, as shown in the
Education Heritage item.
For all the reasons presented in this Registration Dossier and due to its
representative, symbolic, identity, and cultural values, the cultural good "The ET Case
and Varginha's Identity" was indicated for studies and Registered as Intangible Cultural
Heritage of Varginha.

===== PAGE 231 [page-a7fcc318d39ba6740690a181] =====
--- seg-773cf1de4425b680ccb82f69 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-b04fa33563e63df96b1d47a1 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG

6. Opening a new register book - Book of Representations
--- seg-703f004aaefd117176713e8e [needs-review] ---
As per the decision of the Municipal Cultural Heritage Council of Varginha, at its 218th meeting, held on May 18, 2023, the Council decided to open a new Register Book for the inscription of Varginha's intangible cultural heritage. The Register Book of Representations was created in accordance with Article 2, § 2 of Municipal Decree No. 8.818/18.

After several discussions regarding the nature of the asset "The E.T. Case and Varginha's Identity," it was concluded that a new Register Book was necessary due to the diffuse nature of the asset and the characteristics of this Register, where the asset is dispersed among distinct representations based on narratives and ideas. The asset is not a cultural practice, a form of expression, a technique, knowledge, or celebration. Its appropriation occurs in a diffuse manner, unlike other intangible cultural heritage assets that typically develop and persist within specific social groups and contexts.

UNESCO defines intangible cultural heritage as "the practices, representations, expressions, knowledge, and techniques—together with the instruments, objects, artifacts, and cultural spaces associated with them—that communities, groups, and in some cases individuals recognize as an integral part of their cultural heritage." This definition is outlined in Article 2 of the Convention for the Safeguarding of Intangible Cultural Heritage.

The term "representation" derives from the Latin term 'repraesentare'—to make present or to present anew.

For some authors, such as Francisco Falcon (1985), the term representation, within the concepts of social sciences or history, lies at the center of a constellation of very diverse notions or concepts, such as imagination(s), ideology(s), myth(s), and mythology(s), utopia(s), and memory(s).

The idea of Representation presented here follows the definitions and concepts of Carlo Ginzburg in the book Wooden Eyes, and the study of Roger Chartier, The World as Representation, which is found in the book On the Edge of the Cliff.
--- seg-4090af0118fbfc6575234f32 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | ZIP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 232 [page-154fbe4d1b22111321cc7a4d] =====
--- seg-9d06505420fc5664ddaf1e97 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-ec0c3c50bca204791ead370a [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-6f6c012603e031234f3ada8a [needs-review] ---
Ginzburg understands that, on one hand, "representation" substitutes for the reality represented, thus evoking absence. On the other hand, it makes the represented reality visible, thus suggesting presence. He calls this movement "a boring game of mirrors," as the opposition could easily be reversed: in the first case, representation is present, albeit as a substitute; in the second, it ultimately refers, by contrast, to the absent reality it seeks to represent (GINZBURG, p. 85). Absence and presence are always a duality, a pair, in representations.

Ginzburg believes that an image is both presence and a substitute for something that is not present. In certain contexts, representation takes the place of the thing represented. In the case of the Varginha ET, regardless of whether it actually existed or not, what we always have is the representation of the event. Not the event, which is past, which existed at a specific moment and was experienced by a limited group of people. The issue of representation in this case serves for a historical event, verifiable, plausible, but also for a work of fiction, for myths, beliefs, among others. For example, the Yeti (considered the abominable snowman) existed or is a product of human imagination? Whether it actually existed or not, it never prevented various representations, narratives, and even concrete searches from occurring to locate the mysterious monster of the snow-covered mountains. In this case, as with the Varginha ET, representations took the place of the thing represented and took on a much greater proportion than the Yeti itself, regardless of whether it existed or not.

For Chartier, the representation relationship should be understood as a relationship between a present image and an absent object, one substituting for the other because it is its homolog. Once again, the issue of presence/absence is raised. Both Chartier and French sociologist Pierre Bourdier understand that representations are social constructions of historical experiences. These two theorists, Chartier and Bourdier, revisit the discussion of Representation by Marcel Mauss and Émile Durkheim.

It is in the sense of representation as something that substitutes for the thing represented and creates new representations, versions, and meanings that we understand the opening of this new Register Book.

Chartier understands that the concept of representation is also at the center of the construction of social identities. Based on Ginzburg, Chartier wrote that in the formation of these identities, representations play a crucial role.
--- seg-401e238cfe4deced0b215a03 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 232 of 403

===== PAGE 233 [page-29190c14e1a74ef30436ec11] =====
--- seg-2b324d8a18e340fee3f94455 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-4d8f342d8028bb9a969c9512 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-5ccb2d0458ae3acb7636d752 [needs-review] ---
Social identities, there is always a power relationship between the representations imposed by those who hold the power to classify and name, and on the other side, the definition, acceptance, or resistance that each community produces of itself. In this power relationship, the representations of the Varginha ET Case emerged and persisted in the collective imagination, even with the denial of authorities such as the Army, Fire Department, Military Police, and also the local government, which only in the 2000s recognized and highlighted the Varginha ET story. Despite jokes and public incredulity, the fact persisted because a community, local or external, produced narratives and representations that resonated the Case and continued to produce new narratives to keep it alive in collective memory.

Mário Ribeiro Duarte (2009) wrote that "the Varginha ET can be understood as the creation of a local tradition, configured from elements derived from literature, cinema, and the internet, ultimately from a distant culture that was incorporated/adapted to the local." For him, the space became global, we are all integrated, connected, and therefore, we participate, influence, and are influenced by what happens from the frozen steppes of Siberian Russia, through Alaska, New Mexico, Machu Picchu, to Varginha (DUARTE, 2009).

Duarte (2009) also states that the "Varginha ET" created a local market, while simultaneously inserting the city into a much larger market than what existed before. Varginha was publicized and recognized worldwide as the city of the ET. Thus, creating a local identification using a symbolism from global imagination. Here, once again, Varginha is taken as "the city of the ET," a personal pronoun. The ET is from Varginha because Varginha is the city of the ET. He is from Varginha, from the territory of the municipality of southern Minas Gerais, and from no other place.

Given the multiplicity of representations of this memory and collective experiences around the Varginha ET Case, as well as the completely diffuse character of its materialization, we identify that the currently existing Register Books are incapable of encompassing the nuances of this new category of intangible cultural asset that proposes itself.

Therefore, for a better description, we suggest that the new Register Book not be created exclusively for the registration of the asset that led to its conceptualization and origin, but rather serve to register assets related to imagination, memory, ideologies, legends, etc.

to myths, beliefs and identities that have diffuse and distinct forms of appropriation,

For all these explicit reasons, the Register of Representations Book was created
under the understanding that the good is a representation, a narrative, an imagination
deeply rooted in collective memory and materialized in various objects, drawings,
architecture, cartoons, audiovisual, among others. These materializations and narratives
made possible the creation of new representations and ideas that ensured the permanence
and continuity of the good.
Thus, in the Register of Representations Book, should be inscribed the goods
related to imagination, memory, ideologies, legends, myths, beliefs and identities,
that have diffuse and distinct forms of appropriation, expression and recreation.

7. Community consent and involvement, and interviews

Due to the diffuse nature of the good, it is complex to determine who the holders are. A priori, every resident of Varginha would be a holder of the cultural good, as they reside in the municipality. However, it is possible to identify groups with stronger connections to the good, such as artisans gathered in local associations: Minas Gerais Craft Association (AMINARTE); Association of Popular Artisans and Artists of Varginha - ASSOART; the Association of Varginha Retired and Pensioners - AAPV, and the Varginha Creative Economy Association - AVEC. As well as cartoonists, illustrators, designers, artists, merchants, musicians, representatives of entities like the Gerais Paths Circuit, journalists, documentarians, teachers, architects, ufologists, among others.

In addition to these directly related to the creation of representations and the maintenance of living memory and the possibilities of the good's continuity, the local government, represented by the Mayor, Secretaries, and employees of the Secretariats, are also important holders who will enable the implementation of the good's Safeguarding.

Throughout the Registration process, various individuals were interviewed and directly involved in the studies, providing accounts, indicating other people, making descriptions, and demonstrating how the good has remained latent over time.

Below, we include the signed consents and, likewise, the signature lists from petitions collected throughout the municipality.

===== PAGE 341 [page-ec8d68d01b55599a8a882ea3] =====
--- seg-b7348632e991e3791b6c4d2a [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-95a58b2048c514c0509060f0 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-f7855fc21298d5999ae84c86 [needs-review] ---
Twenty-five years ago, Brazil was introduced to the mysterious case of the Varginha ET
BY HISTORY CHANNEL BRAZIL ON JANUARY 20, 2021 AT 2:52 PM
Exactly 25 years ago, on January 20, 1996, Brazil was surprised by the Varginha ET case, an incident
that allegedly occurred in the interior of Minas Gerais. It all started when three girls from that city claimed
to have seen a creature with brown, sticky skin, large red eyes, and a bulky head with protrusions. Other
witnesses at the time also reported seeing an UFO in the area.
According to rumors, an UFO had crashed at the site and a dead alien had been taken to Campinas for an
autopsy on its body. The local firefighters and military police denied all the information. To this day, there
is no evidence that extraterrestrial beings visited the Minas Gerais interior. Nevertheless, the story became
an urban legend and the Varginha ET entered national folklore.
But what would be the true origin of the appearance? According to a 1997 investigation, a great
misunderstanding gave rise to the Varginha ET. The girls, in reality, encountered a man known as Mudinho,
who lived with his family in front of the supposed sighting location. This man, suffering from mental
illness, had the habit of crouching down to collect small objects from the ground.
--- seg-1b765bfdf7de6ffebb855473 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | ZIP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718
--- seg-1e4cf78db59e9a18c25452d2 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Page 341 of 403

===== PAGE 342 [page-e2d3a8efd575713184ebe382] =====
--- seg-69c23da7b36bb1cf87289a20 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-1f714df17afd47f115f93d26 [needs-review] ---
Varginha Municipality | MG
Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-0064a1376c841bc797e5645d [machine-unreviewed] ---
On the day of the incident, Mudinho might have been covered in mud due to the heavy rains and scared the girls
who mistook him for an alien creature.
Although the alien presence was ruled out, the city of Varginha receives many tourists because of the
story. In fact, one of the city's tourist attractions is a large water tank in the center, in the shape of a spaceship.

Varginha ET: Case turns 20 years old with mysteries and uncertainties
Three girls reportedly saw an extraterrestrial in January 1996. The city became worldwide known
for the case, which was never clarified.
Varginha, January 20, 1996. It would have been a normal day in the quiet town of 100,000 inhabitants in
Southern Minas, but the account of three girls changed everything. They said they saw a strange, short
creature with red eyes, who didn't seem of this world. From day to night, researchers and countless
witnesses started talking about the "Varginha ET".
More reports emerged about unidentified creatures and objects, military operations, strange movements
in the city, and mysterious deaths. The case was featured in UFO publications around the world. In these
20 years, many doubts remained in the air. But the fact is that the story is part of Brazilian imagination and
terrified many people. Recall the main

In the 20th anniversary of the Varginha ET, artists present new images
The alleged extraterrestrial sighting marks two decades of mystery. To celebrate the anniversary, artists
create their versions of the ET.
The alleged Varginha ET sighting, which turns 20 this Wednesday (20), captivates the imagination of many. In
the days following the reports of the three girls who allegedly saw the creature in an empty lot, many artists
reproduced images of what the supposed extraterrestrial might look like based on their descriptions.
To commemorate two decades of mystery surrounding the Varginha ET, G1 asked new artists to depict what
they think the supposed ET would look like in drawings or cartoons.
"It's cool to work with a story that's in the Brazilian imagination, play with a case that has been raising so
many questions for two decades. Especially for those, like me, who grew up in Pouso Alegre, in the same
region as Varginha. I hope internet users enjoy the cartoon as much as I did creating it," said journalist
Jarbas Soares, who thinks that after so much interaction with humans, the ET might even like cheese bread.

The Varginha Case, a UFO incident considered the most notable of its kind in all of Brazil's history, marks its 15th anniversary in January. Located in southern Minas Gerais, Varginha became a global hotspot for ufology after three girls reported facing an extraterrestrial being. The fascination with the story, beyond the direct third-degree contact, centered around a claim made by the UFO community, which organized a pilgrimage to the Minas Gerais city. According to them, the Brazilian Army, Military Police, and Fire Department captured two creatures from another planet on January 20, 1996, and took them for analysis in hospitals and a necropsy in Campinas, SP. These institutions, ufologists claim, have since withheld the disclosure of these events.

The Varginha City Hall inaugurates E.T. Memorial
The space is already a visitor attraction and also features a planetarium that will show educational films
The Varginha City Hall is delivering to the population this week one of the largest tourist attractions, the
E.T. Memorial along with the Planetarium, located on the top of Vila Paiva where the Municipal Tourism and Commerce Secretariat is operating.
The E.T. Memorial is named after "Radialista Galvão Conde", a deceased journalist in 2017 at 84 years old, who dedicated his life to communication and worked to promote Varginha's name through radio waves.
The Planetarium will show short 360° educational and cultural films.
With an inauguration scheduled for this Tuesday, November 8th at 7 pm, these spaces promise to be attractions due to the great success of the VARGINHA E.T. case, which will have its story told through suspended panels and videos on nine televisions installed on playful E.T. hands, created by artist Gleiber Piva.
The City Hall states that it is a topic that touches the imagination of many people and invites scholars, researchers, and ufologists to join this special visit to Varginha, internationally known for the UFO episode.
The event will feature the presence of ufologists Vitório Pacaccini, Marco Aurelio, Jamil Villanova, and others of renown in international ufology.
The young women who witnessed the creatures in the afternoon of January 20, 1996, were also invited.
E.T. Memorial
The E.T. Memorial project was announced in 2008, with the work remaining stalled for years. In 2013, when Antônio Silva and Vérdi Melo took office, they committed to completing the work.
Now, under the Vérdi administration, the monument is being delivered to the public.

The ET Memorial will be inaugurated on the evening of this Tuesday (8), in the Vila Paiva neighborhood, in Varginha (MG). The project was announced in 2008, was even halted, and now, after 14 years, it will be delivered to the population.

In addition to a tourist attraction, the site will have an educational purpose with a planetarium, which will show educational films and the history of the Varginha ET case. The story will be told through suspended panels and videos on nine televisions installed in the hands of playful ET figures, created by artist Gleiber Piva.

The event will feature the presence of the three girls, now women, who witnessed the creatures on January 20, 1996, as well as ufologists Vitório Pacaccini, Marco Aurélio, Jamil Villanova, and others of international renown.

===== PAGE 357 [page-4bcfe1bff2df4f7c474ddcf8] =====
--- seg-da884b36d5ee573fa8b5dd6f [needs-review] ---

The Varginha City Hall is conducting studies to register the "Varginha ET Case" as an Intangible Cultural Heritage Dossier
In March 2023, the Varginha Cultural Heritage Council accepted requests from the local community to initiate studies for a municipal registration dossier related to the "Varginha ET Case."
The "Varginha Case," as the events that occurred in January 1996, involving unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings and possible mysterious non-human beings, became known, made Varginha a globally recognized "ET town."
Since these episodes, reported by local residents and subsequent UFO studies, widely disseminated by national and international media, Varginha has organized itself over the past 20 years, shaping its narrative as the "ET town."
Various actions have been taken to construct this image, such as the projection of architectural and urban furniture that directly allude to the representation of what is imagined to be an extraterrestrial creature and unidentified flying objects, commonly known as "flying saucers."
Recently, American filmmaker James Fox released the documentary "Moment of Contact" (or "The Varginha Case: Moment of Contact") which demonstrates that the story remains alive, fostering discussions, new reports, and interest to this day, preserving the memory of the 1996 events.
In this context of the strong identity created in the municipality of Varginha related to the ET and UFO history, the registration is placed.
--- seg-c4dcf7218c38d26f9b70471a [needs-review] ---

The registration as local Intangible Heritage will enable preservation actions focused on patrimonial education directed towards astronomy studies, spatial knowledge, and ufology.
“The ET case made our city world-known. Today, we receive visitors from all over, scholars of the case spread the Varginha incident, and it is up to us, public managers, to take care of this illustrious visitor who chose our city to land,” said Mayor Vérdi Melo.

Brazil Beyond: 'Abducted' in Varginha, Boa Esporte gains a new name and ET
Disputed by municipalities, former Ituiutaba changes address and adds the symbol of the new city to increase
local identification. 08/03/2011 12:20 - Updated on 11/15/2011 14:46
Brazil Beyond: 'Abducted' in Varginha, Boa Esporte gains a new name and ET
Disputed by municipalities, former Ituiutaba changes address and adds the symbol of the new city to increase
local identification. By Thiago de Lima, Rio de Janeiro
On January 20, 1996, the story in Varginha, a city in southern Minas Gerais, goes that on that date, after
UFO (Unidentified Flying Object) sightings over the region, three teenagers allegedly encountered an
extraterrestrial being, which was later captured and kept secret by the Brazilian Army. The case made headlines
around the world and became the most famous alien phenomenon in the country. Fifteen years later, like an UFO,
the Boa Esporte Clube landed in the city with the name of another Minas Gerais municipality, Ituiutaba, and
without identification with the local population. But to gain the sympathy of the residents, in addition to
changing the way it was called, the team adopted a new mascot: Varginha's ET.
Varginha Municipality | Mayor: Verdi Lúcio Melo | Sector Head: Danielle de Souza Guimarães
Responsible Sector: Technical Coordination of Cultural Heritage
Praça Matheus Tavares, 121 | Neighborhood: Center | ZIP: 37002-320 | Tel.: (35) 3690-2718

===== PAGE 362 [page-b8089f1474ceba620f77ef4d] =====
--- seg-e2b31ecadb01880941cfe4b1 [needs-review] ---
Registration Process of immaterial good: "The E.T. Case and the Vargas Identity"
--- seg-2212889c59a7f350bce907a9 [needs-review] ---

Year 2023 | Exercise 2025
--- seg-f672db2e39c3f187cda22b98 [needs-review] ---
Next to the owl, the official symbol of the club, the extraterrestrial enters the field in all Boa Esporte games at the Prefeito Dilzon Luiz de Melo Stadium, popularly known as Melão, in Varginha. With a capacity of 15,400 people, the stadium was the first reason for the many changes that were to come. Still named Ituiutaba Esporte Clube, the team played its matches at the Fazendinha Stadium in Ituiutaba, which currently holds around 1,800 people. But after finishing as runner-up in last year's Série C and qualifying for the national second division, which requires a minimum of 15,000 seats in stadiums, the team found itself without a place to host its games, a fact that sparked Varginha's interest.

10. Safeguard and Diagnosis Plan for Cultural Heritage at Registration

The intangible heritage identified as "The E.T. Case and the Varginha Identity" is alive and remains active in the representations of Varginha Municipality. Research for the dossier preparation identified that the history of the alleged extraterrestrial appearance continues to be alive, fostering discussions, new reports, and interest until today. All these factors keep the memory of the events that occurred in 1996 alive and allows for new recreations and the continuity of the heritage.

However, it is not the event itself, known as the Varginha E.T. Case, that we want to protect and safeguard, but the representations and narratives that were made around the history and that projected Varginha onto the global ufology scene. These representations created an identity for Varginha Municipality, which "gained" an E.T., as well as narratives and discourses.

We identified that there was first an outward movement regarding the Varginha E.T. Case, and only later did the municipality recognize itself in that narrative. Some citizens, like artisan Mauri Bomfim, made the first representations in large statues and sculptures immediately after the event, still in 1996. After him, dozens of people followed, making various representations present in crafts, buildings, urban furniture, sculptures, cartoons, drawings, among others.

The persistence of the Varginha E.T. Case in the media, in monuments and buildings, in cartoons, in crafts, among others, kept a collective memory alive. This memory projected Varginha beyond its borders, with the municipality's image linked to the E.T. idea.

The goal of Registration and the construction of a Safeguard Plan is for astronomy to be explored along with spatial and universe knowledge, encouraging and fostering Patrimonial Education. Similarly, these actions aim to encourage ufology studies in the municipality. The Varginha Cultural Foundation Archive also intends to create a fund on the Varginha E.T. Case and all the representations made about it.

These actions will further promote the theme, benefiting holders dispersed among artisans, merchants, among others.

1. Creation of a documentary collection on the "Varginha E.T. Case"

4. Dialogue between holders and the Varginha Cultural Foundation
Holders here are understood as all those directly or indirectly related to the Varginha ET Case and who create and recreate representations about it. Due to the diffuse nature of the good, holders are specified in a specific item of the Registration Dossier and signed the consents and the below-signed.

6. Continuous dialogue between the Varginha Cultural Foundation and the Tourism Secretariat, responsible for the ET Memorial

7. Identification of the good "The E.T. Case and Varginha's Identity" as Intangible
Heritage of the municipality
It is important that the good "The E.T. Case and Varginha's Identity" be publicized and
diffused as part of the local protected patrimonial goods. With the Registration, the
highlighted good becomes part of the Cultural Heritage of the Varginhense people.
It should be encouraged that all publications on Social Networks, in newspapers or
periodicals, include the information that the representations and materializations of
the Varginha E.T. Case are intangible heritage of the Varginhense people.
--- seg-e6e1a28b0176db6a7f6cbea7 [needs-review] ---
8. Possibilities of using FUMPAC resources for promoting, producing and distributing associated products

The promotion of well-being and integration between the Cultural Foundation of Varginha and the Municipal Tourism Secretariat require actions such as producing brochures, audiovisuals, and various dissemination materials.
--- seg-5295c96220fdcf1cf492e2e7 [needs-review] ---
9. Annual creation of a week dedicated to the theme of the Varginha ET Case
Create an annual event where the themes of the Varginha ET Case, astronomy, and
ufology are in the spotlight and can be discussed and presented, mainly in
patrimonial education events at the ET Memorial. On these occasions, the planetarium
can show films about astronomy and ufology. In schools, teachers can discuss the
themes of astronomy and knowledge of the universe.
